# Community Balance Patch: every change

| of which held for review (not in the pack) | 0 |

Rules: tolerance ±0.15 on the residual (one robust sigma); power moves at most ×1.20 per unit in one pass (stats), the rest goes to price, which moves at most ×1.20; stats move for melee cavalry, melee infantry, missile cavalry, missile infantry, monstrous cavalry, monstrous infantry; price alone moves for chariot, monster, war beast, warmachine. Attack and defence move by 9 × ln k with the damage-and-HP factor k. **drift** is 1 − cosine similarity of the matchup-and-share vector before and after (0 = fights exactly the same way).

Identity drift over the 370 changed units: mean 0.0000, max 0.0011 (Infernal Ironsworn).
